# Open questions (ROADMAP 4.3)

1327 questions raised by the archive's core claims: 1223 open, 82 partly-answered, 22 answered. Each lists what is known (S-ids are records, C-ids are claims; the host's claims are not evidence), what would settle it, and the claims that raise it. Generated by `scripts/questions/apply_register.py` from `open_questions.json`.

## Donor pressure on Kirk

### Q-6 · open · 62 claims (2025-09-15 to 2026-07-27)
**Did donors or pro-Israel figures pressure Charlie Kirk and TPUSA in 2023-2025 over Israel or over whom he hosted and associated with (such as Tucker Carlson, Candace Owens or Dave Smith), including by threatening or withdrawing funds, and who were they?**
- **Known:** The host says Kirk was under donor pressure from October 7, 2023 (C-11289, C-11370, C-11436) and that donors threatened to withdraw money and dictated speakers, with pressure peaking before the shooting (C-74, C-75, C-96, C-97, C-233, C-467, C-471, C-474, C-475). She says Kirk felt 'morally blackmailed', including over the Hamptons and his friendship with her, and planned to bring her back (C-713, C-714, C-717, C-718, C-758); that donors demanded he bar her from AmFest and denounce her, that he refused, and that she will name the donor who yelled at him (C-2791, C-2890, C-3130, C-3136, C-3144). She names Seth Dillon, Josh Hammer and Bob Shillman as angry after Carlson's 2025-07-11 speech, says a donor confronted Kirk backstage, that Shillman withdrew money, that Jewish and Christian Zionist donors threatened him financially, that pro-Israel actors pulled money and Shapiro used donor connections against him, and that the pressure made Kirk ill (C-1136, C-1517, C-1536, C-2050, C-2127, C-2128, C-2187, C-2213, C-2221, C-2352, C-2657, C-4957, C-5320, C-5525, C-5526, C-6905, C-6967, C-7099); Megyn Kelly is said to have described the pressure (C-1561, C-2227). She says Bowyer told her TPUSA will always be pro-Israel (C-2126), that Erika Kirk faces the same pressure (C-2061), that Kirk's death served Netanyahu's war aims (C-5010), and that those close to Kirk will not name the donors (C-1065). The record says nothing yet.
- **Would settle it:** Dated donor and staff correspondence with Kirk from late 2023 through 2025-09-10; TPUSA donor records and 990s before and after the July 2025 Student Action Summit; Hammer's and Dillon's posts of July 11-13; AmFest speaker decisions; on-record accounts from those named, Kelly, Carlson and TPUSA staff.
- **Claims:** C-74, C-75, C-96, C-97, C-233, C-275, C-467, C-471, C-473, C-474, C-475, C-542 …

## Mikey McCoy call sequence

### Q-212 · open · 60 claims (2025-10-27 to 2026-10-08)
**Was Mikey McCoy already on a call at the moment of the shot, and whom did he call afterwards (Elizabeth McCoy, Erika Kirk, Rob McCoy, a third participant 'Danny', 911), in what order, at what times and for how long?**
- **Known:** The host says his phone reached his ear 834 ms after the shot without visible dialling, so he must already have been on a call (C-1735, C-1737, C-1740, C-1906). Accounts of the order conflict. Erika Kirk says he called her within seconds (C-6197, C-7909, C-11079), the NYT describes McCoy's number appearing (C-7907), TPUSA first said he called Erika immediately (C-12540, C-12577), and the host says the public impression was that Erika was called first (C-4102). Blake Neff says McCoy called Erika within a minute and then his father Rob McCoy to rally pastors (C-4100, C-7318, C-12693, C-12694, C-14061, C-14062, C-14063), and describes him taking command after a call and saying Erika would hear only from him (C-2372, C-2374); Rob McCoy said Mikey called him first, which the host calls a lie and says Mikey attributed to his father's confusion (C-2382, C-3987). Citing call logs TPUSA showed her, the host says McCoy called his wife Elizabeth first and conferenced Erika in about three minutes later for under two minutes, then his father, then had a 10-minute call with Danny Phillip between 12:24 and 12:44, and that none of his early calls was to 911 (C-2235, C-2377, C-4098, C-4103, C-4108, C-6109, C-6196, C-6198, C-6199, C-7435, C-7941, C-7942, C-11078, C-11080, C-11085, C-11087, C-12097, C-12098, C-12099). She first named the third participant as Mikey's brother Danny McCoy, before the shot, then corrected it to Danny, another of Charlie's assistants (C-4107, C-4110, C-4123). She has also asked for the logs (C-2733), speculated that he called Elizabeth and arranged the routing of Kirk's plane (C-3099, C-3104), and said the calls' timing is being misrepresented (C-2661, C-2667); Turek says McCoy called who needed calling (C-7599). The record says nothing yet.
- **Would settle it:** Authenticated carrier call-detail records with second-level timestamps and subscriber information for Mikey McCoy's, Elizabeth McCoy's, Erika Kirk's, Rob McCoy's and Danny Phillip's phones on 2025-09-10 (12:15-13:30 MT), set against the timestamped shot; the call-log exhibit the host showed; Utah County 911 logs; frame-accurate footage with audio sync; the earliest TPUSA and NYT accounts.
- **Claims:** C-1730, C-1735, C-1737, C-1740, C-1906, C-2235, C-2372, C-2374, C-2376, C-2377, C-2382, C-2402 …

## other participants

- **Known:** The host repeatedly says Robinson did not act alone: more people were involved, a 'hive', a military or deep-state operation, possibly armed people in the audience, and that she is 99.9 percent certain (C-122, C-191, C-636, C-738, C-754, C-773, C-786, C-816, C-1654, C-1791, C-1792, C-1882, C-1920, C-1958, C-2057, C-2402, C-2403, C-2404, C-2418, C-3674, C-3677, C-4017, C-4024, C-7635, C-11907, C-11940). Carlson says sole action must be proven (C-3922, C-3930); the host says Kash Patel told Megyn Kelly Robinson was the only person involved (C-3745). The record says nothing yet on co-participants.
- **Would settle it:** Charging documents and any superseding charges; FBI and Utah investigative findings on co-conspirators; Robinson's device extractions, Discord, phone, location and financial records (including contacts on 2025-09-09/10); camera footage; trial and hearing evidence.

## Camera and SD card removal

### Q-63 · open · 46 claims (2025-09-19 to 2026-09-01)
**Who removed the event cameras and SD cards after the shot (including the camera behind Kirk), when and in what order, on whose instruction and why, and what is the cards' chain of custody?**
- **Known:** The host says a TPUSA-linked man she knows, whom she identifies as AV staffer Terryl Farnsworth (the man in the background of the staff call), sat to Kirk's left, took down the cameras to give footage to the FBI, consulted TPUSA lawyers and showed her the footage over FaceTime (C-502, C-504, C-506, C-508, C-509, C-510, C-513, C-514, C-515, C-591, C-5725). On timing, the host says the rear camera came down first, about four minutes after the shot and about three minutes after the yellow camera fell, after Farnsworth recorded himself announcing Kirk's death, while he stood on Kirk's chair looking around (C-1127, C-1355, C-1643, C-1770, C-2366, C-7686, C-7848, C-12210, C-12211, C-12239, C-12240, C-12284, C-12297); she says footage of the quick removal fuels suspicion (C-867). Kolvet says the team grabbed the SD cards (plural) so law enforcement could get them, that they are with the FBI, that Farnsworth is a long-time friend, and that he took them because people can be evil (C-712, C-1357, C-12229, C-12231, C-12232, C-12292); the host says footage shows only the rear camera's card taken, after which he texted rather than collecting the others (C-12238, C-12244, C-12582). On instruction, Tyler Bowyer first said police ordered the takedown (C-1367); the host calls that a lie, says Farnsworth told her no police officer instructed him, rejects Kolvet's hat-theft and item-protection explanations, and says Farnsworth told her he wanted to keep Erika from seeing the footage (C-1644, C-1761, C-2662, C-2663, C-3994, C-3995, C-7688, C-7690, C-12245). No record yet.
- **Would settle it:** Original time-coded footage of all camera positions after the shot; Farnsworth's, Kolvet's and Bowyer's statements to investigators; police and FBI records of any instruction; evidence logs and chain of custody for each card.
- **Claims:** C-502, C-504, C-506, C-508, C-509, C-510, C-513, C-514, C-515, C-591, C-712, C-867 …
- **Tips:** C-502

## How Robinson surrendered

### Q-34 · partly-answered · 45 claims (2025-09-15 to 2026-09-02)
**How did Tyler Robinson come to surrender on 2025-09-11: who learned he had been identified, who contacted whom, what roles did his parents and the family friend (Mike Mitchell) play, was a raid or SWAT action threatened, and did he confess to anyone beforehand?**
- **Known:** Agents Hull (S-3, 01:51:41) and Davis (S-5, 02:50:14; S-4, 37:00-37:16) testified Robinson went voluntarily to the Washington County Sheriff's Office in St. George with his parents and a family friend; the bindover ruling found the friend picked him up to take him there and quotes a 2025-09-11 Discord message, 'I'm surrendering through a sheriff friend in a few moments' (S-7 07:55:06). The Information says a family friend told him to bring evidence to the sheriff's office (S-1 p. 7), and texts mention a neighbour who was a deputy (S-1 p. 9, S-5 02:49:25). The host and Sheriff Brooksby's clips give competing accounts of the call and what Robinson was told (C-753, C-961, C-962, C-963, C-970, C-972, C-974, C-975, C-977, C-1061); Brooksby described a friend arranging a voluntary surrender (C-7641, C-7642). The host's sources say the friend, Mike Mitchell, told the family to bring him in the easy way or face SWAT, that the parents did not turn him in, and that he went peaceably with his father (C-7627, C-7629, C-7637, C-10609, C-10610, C-11759, C-11831, C-11841, C-11968, C-12641, C-12654). She disputes that the family recognised the grandfather's rifle from a news photo and says Brooksby admitted Robinson was pressed to turn himself in (C-8368, C-8369, C-8371, C-8372); she relays a story that his father turned him in after speaking with a pastor (C-130), notes a clip's account of driving to Orem (C-7397), questions officials' account (C-3999, C-4002), and says no confession or parental turn-in was proven at the hearing (C-10633, C-11441). Whether he confessed and the father's exact words are not settled.
- **Would settle it:** Washington County Sheriff's surrender reports, intake, contact and call logs (including the 8:02 p.m. call); Brooksby's full press-conference recording; statements and testimony of the parents, Mike Mitchell, the deputy and any pastor; interview recordings; the charging documents' surrender narrative and phone records.
- **Claims:** C-130, C-185, C-188, C-209, C-210, C-753, C-949, C-961, C-962, C-963, C-964, C-970 …

### Q-2 · open · 42 claims (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-14)

- **Known:** The host says Kirk was undergoing a change of heart, had many doubts known to his inner circle, wavered in his own words, said he did not want the money, and was done with the pro-Israel cause, and that she, Kelly and Carlson described this accurately from their own talks with him (C-60, C-62, C-100, C-275, C-1131, C-1142, C-1316, C-1556, C-2164, C-2220, C-2221, C-2325, C-2650, C-3989, C-3453, C-6464, C-6568, C-6501, C-6906, C-6909, C-6966, C-7080, C-7199, C-7275, C-7335, C-8846, C-8849, C-8850, C-9605, C-9674, C-14223); a clip shows Kirk saying pro-Israel behaviour was pushing people like him away (C-260), and a viewer says he abandoned the cause the day before (C-14272). ## Erika Kirk Fort Huachuca alibi

### Q-441 · open · 38 claims (2026-01-05 to 2026-05-11)
**Was Erika Kirk at Fort Huachuca (including the Candlewood Suites) on 2025-09-08 or 09-09, where was she on those days, and what alibi has her side offered, to whom and on what documented basis?**
- **Known:** The host relays witness Mitch Snow saying he saw her leaving the hotel on the 8th with a Cabot Phillips lookalike, not at the meeting on the 9th, and that he remains certain (C-4465, C-4466, C-4470, C-4564, C-6338, C-6513); Snow's credibility was attacked publicly (C-6337). - **Would settle it:** Fort Huachuca gate, visitor, access and lodging records and Candlewood Suites registration and camera footage for 2025-09-08/09; flight manifests and travel records; Erika Kirk's phone location data; dated, geotagged photos or video; the original deleted post; the unredacted alibi message and photo with metadata, the chain of who sent it, and the Paramount Tactical segment where it was read.

## Wound path and exit

### Q-64 · answered · 36 claims (2025-09-19 to 2026-04-02)
**Where did the bullet enter Charlie Kirk's body, what path did it take, did it exit, and where in his body was it or its fragments recovered?**
- **Known:** The bindover ruling finds a neck wound with a front-to-back, slightly left-to-right, downward path, no exit wound, and the bullet coming to rest in the tissues of Kirk's upper back (S-7 07:46:29, 08:16:02; C-7027 corroborated), and fragments were recovered at autopsy (S-5). The host says the bullet entered the front left lower neck and the shot did not come from behind (C-1661, C-1704); she earlier relayed experts calling the front wound an exit wound (C-511, C-518) and argued with a clip guest (Range Day Bro) about blood, a rear entry and an exit (C-592, C-907, C-908, C-910). Accounts of where it was found shifted: Kolvet said the coroner found it just beneath the skin (C-595, C-691, C-1613), and later told the host it travelled from C2 to C6 into the thorax, with other versions naming the stomach (C-7707, C-7708, C-7939). The host says the videos show little blood and no exit, inconsistent with a .30-06, disputes the 'strong bones', 'steel neck' and ricochet explanations offered by Erika Kirk, Kolvet and others (C-3785, C-3978, C-3979, C-3980, C-3988, C-4079, C-4538, C-4571, C-4572, C-6236, C-6237, C-6435, C-6437, C-6755, C-6756, C-7032), and speculates the non-exit was meant to stay secret (C-607, C-7692).
- **Would settle it:** Settled in outline by the ruling; the medical examiner's autopsy report, the trauma surgeon's operative notes and the projectile recovery record with chain of custody would add detail.
- **Claims:** C-511, C-518, C-592, C-595, C-607, C-691, C-907, C-908, C-910, C-1577, C-1609, C-1613 …
- **Tips:** C-3979

## Netanyahu Hamptons call

- **Known:** The host says Netanyahu offered to take TPUSA 'to the next level' (a 'boatload of money', amount unknown) and Kirk declined, that Bill Ackman put Netanyahu on the phone, that this was their final call, and that no one has denied it while TPUSA's and participants' statements ignore it (C-654, C-3455, C-3456, C-3466, C-4813, C-6391, C-6395, C-7758, C-7759, C-7760, C-7761, C-10681); she hopes Erika Kirk will address it (C-652). No record yet.
- **Would settle it:** Kirk's phone records with date and duration; Netanyahu's office's account of the call and the May letter; Ackman's and other attendees' accounts and the attendee list; Kolvet's and Erika Kirk's on-record statements; any written funding proposal or TPUSA donor records.

## fatal shot origin

### Q-27 · partly-answered · 34 claims (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-21)
**Where was the fatal shot fired from, and was it fired from the Losee Center roof?**
- **Known:** Hull and Faumuina testified a person was on the Losee Center roof at the time of the shot (S-3 00:55:27, S-5 04:45:51); the Information alleges a person dropped onto the roof at about 12:15 (S-1 p. 5); Officer Bagley recognised a rifle shot (S-2 01:40:24); and the ruling finds a front-to-back, slightly left-to-right, downward wound path (S-7 07:46:29, 08:16:02), which fixes the direction of entry but not the firing position. Lyman said in hindsight the shot probably came from the roof above him (C-1098), and Neff says the autopsy confirms that direction (C-11528). The host and others dispute it: hunters and military members say another direction (C-512); the host theorises a shot from beneath Kirk or from the audience to his left, says it did not come from behind or the Losee Center, that the rooftop person fired no shot, and favours an exploding microphone (C-607, C-1470, C-1642, C-1660, C-1704, C-7635, C-11575, C-11577); a clip guest argues for a rear shot with a muzzle flash in a window and a figure in a bush (C-906, C-908, C-911, C-913, C-914); O'Neill and the host say witnesses reporting another direction were shut down (C-1463, C-7597); Chaffetz is said to reject the far-shot and Losee theory (C-1648, C-1650).
- **Would settle it:** The medical examiner's wound-trajectory report; ballistic and scene reconstruction against rooftop and seat positions; the rifle, casing and projectile recovery evidence; rooftop camera footage at 12:23; acoustic analysis of the shot.
- **Claims:** C-119, C-165, C-511, C-512, C-607, C-906, C-908, C-911, C-913, C-914, C-1096, C-1098 …
- **Tips:** C-512

## Rifle forensic linkage

### Q-75 · partly-answered · 23 claims (2025-09-22 to 2026-08-10)
**Which rifle was recovered, and what forensic evidence (ballistics, cartridge case, fingerprints, DNA) links it to Tyler Robinson and to the fatal shot, including whether it was fired on 2025-09-10?**
- **Known:** Sgt. Faumuina testified the recovered gun was a Mauser 98 rifle (S-3 05:03:26), and the bindover ruling treats a Mauser .30-06 as the suspected murder weapon (S-7). The ATF report identifies the cartridge case found in the rifle as fired in that rifle (S-5); it does not by itself date the firing. Neff and Kolvet say the rifle came from Robinson's grandfather and bore his DNA (C-7396). The host accepts the rifle was Robinson's heirloom but says it was not fired that day or used to kill Kirk and asks for proof (C-7403, C-7674, C-7711, C-7713, C-7894, C-8343, C-10612), says no evidence connecting it to the crime scene was shown (C-11758), says authorities admitted multiple sets of prints on it (C-6234), and questions the comparison methods; defense counsel says the ATF and FBI examined different objects under different protocols (C-8352, C-8388). She thinks the search was for a different weapon (C-649) and relays a roof witness saying the gun he saw was smaller than the recovered rifle (C-688, C-748); Lyman doubts it was ever on the roof (C-1023). No fingerprint or DNA report is in the cited record.
- **Would settle it:** ATF and FBI firearm and toolmark reports with bench notes; ballistic comparison of the projectile and jacket fragment with the rifle; bore, chamber and residue findings; crime-lab latent-print and DNA reports; recovery-time and chain-of-custody records; roof witnesses' recorded statements; trial exhibits.
- **Claims:** C-649, C-688, C-748, C-1023, C-6234, C-7396, C-7399, C-7403, C-7674, C-7711, C-7713, C-7714 …

## Robinson's alleged motive

### Q-183 · partly-answered · 17 claims (2025-10-08 to 2026-09-02)
**What motive does the prosecution allege for Tyler Robinson's shooting of Charlie Kirk, and what evidence (including his political views, communications and any discussion of Kirk or LGBTQ issues with Lance Twiggs or others) bears on it?**
- **Known:** The State cited a text attributed to Robinson, 'Some hate can't be negotiated out' (S-7, 06:06:16), and Novak argued Twiggs identified as non-gender-conforming and was in a relationship with Robinson (S-7, 05:19:06); the host says the prosecution argues Robinson perceived Kirk's statements as hateful (C-12713), while the defense (Novak) argues the motive is an inference without evidence (C-12707). In his statement played at the hearing, Twiggs said he went by Luna to some people (S-5 02:32:01), that he and Robinson did 'not really' discuss gender identity or LGBTQ rights (S-5 02:31:12), and that he never heard Robinson talk about Charlie Kirk (S-5 02:30:54). The host doubts an LGBTQ motive and that Kirk was killed for angering the trans lobby, says the pre-hearing narrative was a rage over trans rights tied to Twiggs, that Robinson was into gaming, not politics, and came from a Trump-supporting family, and that Twiggs's testimony erases the motive (C-1573, C-10605, C-10611, C-11601, C-11603, C-11835, C-12333, C-12610, C-12705, C-12706). Carlson asks how the suspect was radicalized (C-3932).
- **Would settle it:** The State's motive and political-motivation filings with exhibits, the defense response and the court's ruling; full text, Discord and device extractions and Robinson's online activity; Twiggs's full statement and testimony from family and other witnesses.
- **Claims:** C-1573, C-3932, C-10605, C-10611, C-11455, C-11601, C-11603, C-11618, C-11761, C-11835, C-11836, C-12333 …

## Rooftop figure movements

### Q-26 · partly-answered · 17 claims (2025-09-15 to 2026-10-21)

- **Known:** Agent Hull testified the person stayed at the roof edge until the 12:23:28 shot and then moved north (S-3 00:55:27); Sgt. Faumuina said video showed the shooter run southwest to northeast (S-5 04:45:51); the Information describes the low crawl, the dwell and the controlled drop from the roof (S-1 p. 5); Bagley testified dispatch described the rooftop male running to the edge, dropping down, crawling and going prone (S-2 02:34:29), and that he learned from video at the police department that the man jumped off the building (S-2 04:09:47). A witness says the figure dropped onto the roof about 12:20 rather than the federal 12:15, was in position about 20 seconds, and the shot came about 5 seconds after his recording ended (C-10796, C-10799, C-10801, C-10803). The host questions the timing of the run relative to disassembly (C-119, C-120), says the fleeing figure moves too fast to have gone from a firing crouch to a sprint (C-3282), and describes a video of a man laying down before the shot (C-3284). The cited record does not fix the arrival time.
- **Would settle it:** The original rooftop videos and UVU footage of the roof for 12:10-12:30 with embedded timestamps, and the witness clip's metadata; dispatch audio and CAD log; frame-timed expert analysis; witness statements.
- **Claims:** C-119, C-120, C-167, C-174, C-3282, C-3283, C-3284, C-10796, C-10799, C-10801, C-10803, C-10859 …

## cause of death

### Q-930 · answered · 15 claims (2026-06-25 to 2026-09-22)

- **Known:** The bindover ruling finds he died of a gunshot wound to the neck, that the bullet travelled front to back and downward and did not exit, and that bullet fragments recovered from his body were a .30-caliber class round (S-7 07:46:29, 08:16:02). - **Would settle it:** Settled by the bindover findings; the medical examiner's autopsy report and X-rays, ballistic analysis of the recovered fragments, and FBI/ATF examination of the microphone, transmitter and shirt would confirm.
- **Claims:** C-10521, C-10530, C-10552, C-10585, C-10845, C-10958, C-11048, C-11648, C-11650, C-11655, C-11658, C-11662 …

## Bullet fragments and rifle match

### Q-717 · partly-answered · 12 claims (2026-03-24 to 2026-08-05)
**How many bullet fragments were recovered from Kirk and reached the ATF, and can any be identified to or excluded from the recovered Mauser rifle, or as a .30-06 bullet?**
- **Known:** Examiner Karner testified she received one jacket fragment and four lead fragments (S-5 06:25:53); the ATF report found the lead fragments unsuitable for microscopic comparison and the jacket fragment recovered at autopsy can be neither identified nor excluded as fired from the rifle (S-5 05:31:54), consistent with what the host says authorities state (C-7403, C-7465, C-7732, C-7733). The host says the feds cannot connect the gun to the killing (C-7674, C-7710, C-7834) and that the FBI bullet lead analysis was described as still in progress (C-7725); she says seven fragments were reported but only four reached the ATF, with only 6A usable and its .286-.301 caliber range excluding a .30-06 (C-10646, C-10648, C-11842), a fragment point she later revised (C-11652). Whether any other test links the rifle to the shot is not established.
- **Would settle it:** The full ATF and state crime-lab firearms reports and protocols; the medical examiner's evidence log and the ATF receipt; FBI comparative bullet lead results; Karner's testimony on the jacket fragment's class characteristics and caliber; SAAMI .30-06 dimensions.
- **Claims:** C-7403, C-7465, C-7674, C-7710, C-7725, C-7732, C-7733, C-7834, C-10646, C-10648, C-11652, C-11842

## Charges against Robinson

### Q-41 · answered · 10 claims (2025-09-16 to 2026-09-01)
**What charges does the State bring against Tyler Robinson, what does each count (including Count 6 and the children-present allegation) allege, and does the State seek the death penalty?**
- **Known:** Robinson faces capital aggravated murder, felony discharge of a firearm, two obstruction counts (rifle, clothing), two witness-tampering counts (delete texts; stay silent) and a violent offense in the presence of a child, and the State seeks death (S-7, S-10, S-11, S-1, S-5). The Information lists Count 6 as witness tampering (telling the roommate not to talk to police), with children present as a separate allegation (S-1 p. 4, S-7 05:01:11); the bindover ruling found children under 14 present (S-7 08:31:41); the overview alleges he hid the gun, discarded clothing and told the roommate to delete texts (S-1 p. 3). The host lists the same charges (C-12606, C-12615, C-12616, C-12617, C-12618, C-12619, C-12621), though her reading of Count 6 differs (C-164).
- **Would settle it:** Settled by the Information, the amended information and the bindover ruling.
- **Claims:** C-163, C-164, C-424, C-12606, C-12615, C-12616, C-12617, C-12618, C-12619, C-12621

**Is a non-exiting neck wound consistent with a .30-06 round, and does the wound path square with Kirk's observed movement?**
- **Known:** The bindover ruling records fragments of a .30-caliber class round recovered from Kirk's body (S-7 07:46:29). The host says the official account that a .30-06 was stopped by Kirk's neck is false and that such a round should have passed through and knocked him backward (C-2296, C-2432, C-2614, C-2631, C-13407, C-13893).
- **Would settle it:** The autopsy report on wound path and fragmentation; the state crime-lab ballistics report on the recovered projectile; independent forensic and ballistic expert review; frame-by-frame video analysis.
- **Claims:** C-2296, C-2432, C-2614, C-2631, C-13407, C-13893

## was Kirk shot
